109.         private async void Picker_DevicePickerDismissed(DialDevicePicker sender, object args)
110.         {
111.             //Casting must occur from the UI thread.  This dispatches the casting calls to the UI thread.
112.             // TODO[migrate-007]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
113.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
114.             {
115.                 try
116.                 {
117.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Picker DevicePickerDismissed event fired for device"), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
118. 
119.                     if (activeDialDevice != null)
120.                     {
121.                         // Resume video playback
122.                         this.player.Play();
123.                     }
124.                 }
125.                 catch (Exception ex)
126.                 {
127.                     UnhandledExceptionPage.ShowUnhandledException(ex);
128.                 }

132.         private async void Picker_DisconnectButtonClicked(DialDevicePicker sender, DialDisconnectButtonClickedEventArgs args)
133.         {
134.             //Casting must occur from the UI thread.  This dispatches the casting calls to the UI thread.
135.             // TODO[migrate-008]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
136.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>
137.             {
138.                 try
139.                 {
140.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Picker DisconnectButtonClicked event fired for device '{0}'", activeDeviceInformation.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
141. 
142.                     // Get the DialDevice instance for the selected device
143.                     DialDevice selectedDialDevice = await DialDevice.FromIdAsync(args.Device.Id);
144.                     // Update the picker status
145.                     picker.SetDisplayStatus(selectedDialDevice, DialDeviceDisplayStatus.Connecting);
146. 
147.                     DialApp app = selectedDialDevice.GetDialApp(this.dial_appname_textbox.Text);
148. 
149.                     //Get the current application state
150.                     //DialAppStateDetails stateDetails = await app.GetAppStateAsync();
151. 
152.                     DialAppStopResult result = await app.StopAsync();
153. 
154.                     if (result == DialAppStopResult.Stopped)
155.                     {
156.                         // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
157.                         rootPage.NotifyUser("Application stopped successfully.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
158.                         picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.Device, DialDeviceDisplayStatus.Disconnected);
159.                         activeDialDevice = null;
160.                         activeDeviceInformation = null;
161.                         picker.Hide(); 
162.                     }
163.                     else
164.                     {
165.                         if (result == DialAppStopResult.StopFailed || result == DialAppStopResult.NetworkFailure)
166.                         {
167.                             // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
168.                             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Error occured trying to stop application. Status: '{0}'", result.ToString()), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
169.                             picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.Device, DialDeviceDisplayStatus.Error);
170.                         }
171.                         else //in case of DialAppStopResult.OperationNotSupported, there is not much more you can do. You could implement your own
172.                              // mechanism to stop the application on that device.
173.                         {
174.                             // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
175.                             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Stop is not supported by device: '{0}'", activeDeviceInformation.Name), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
176.                             activeDialDevice = null;    
177.                             activeDeviceInformation = null;
178.                             
179.                         }
180.                     }
181.                 }
182.                 catch (Exception ex)
183.                 {
184.                     UnhandledExceptionPage.ShowUnhandledException(ex);
185.                 }
186. 
187.             });
188.         }
189. 
190.         private async void Picker_DeviceSelected(DialDevicePicker sender, DialDeviceSelectedEventArgs args)
191.         {
192.             //Casting must occur from the UI thread.  This dispatches the casting calls to the UI thread.
193.             // TODO[migrate-009]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
194.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>            
195.             {
196.                 try {
197.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Picker DeviceSelected event fired"), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
198. 
199.                     // Set the status to connecting
200.                     picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.SelectedDialDevice, DialDeviceDisplayStatus.Connecting);
201. 
202.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Resolving DialDevice'"), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
203.              
204.                     //Get the DialApp object for the specific application on the selected device
205.                     DialApp app = args.SelectedDialDevice.GetDialApp(this.dial_appname_textbox.Text);
206. 
207.                     if (app == null)
208.                     {
209.                         //Try to create a DIAL device. If it doesn't succeed, the selected device does not support DIAL.
210.                         //rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("'{0}' cannot find app with ID '{1}'", selectedDeviceInformation.Name, this.dial_appname_textbox.Text), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
211.                         picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.SelectedDialDevice, DialDeviceDisplayStatus.Error);
212.                     }
213.                     else
214.                     {
215.                         rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Attempting to launch '{0}'", app.AppName), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
216.                         //Launch the application on the 1st screen device
217.                         DialAppLaunchResult result = await app.RequestLaunchAsync(this.dial_launch_args_textbox.Text);
218. 
219.                         //Verify to see whether the application was launched
220.                         if (result == DialAppLaunchResult.Launched)
221.                         {
222.                             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Launched '{0}'", app.AppName), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
223.                             activeDialDevice = args.SelectedDialDevice;
224.                             DeviceInformation selectedDeviceInformation = await DeviceInformation.CreateFromIdAsync(args.SelectedDialDevice.Id);
225. 
226.                             activeDeviceInformation = selectedDeviceInformation;
227. 
228.                             //This is where you will need to add you application specific communication between your 1st and 2nd screen applications
229.                             //...
230.                             //...
231.                             //...
232. 
233.                             picker.SetDisplayStatus(activeDialDevice, DialDeviceDisplayStatus.Connected);
234.                             picker.Hide();
235.                         }
236.                         else
237.                         {
238.                             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Attempting to launch '{0}'", app.AppName), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
239.                             picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.SelectedDialDevice, DialDeviceDisplayStatus.Error);
240.                         }
241.                     }
242.                 }
243.                 catch (Exception ex)
244.                 {
245.                     UnhandledExceptionPage.ShowUnhandledException(ex);
246.                 }
247.             });
248.         }

12. using ScreenCasting.Data.Azure;
13. using ScreenCasting.Data.Common;
14. using ScreenCasting.Util;
15. using System;
16. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;
17. using Windows.Storage;
18. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
19. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
20. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
21. 
22. namespace ScreenCasting
23. {
24.     public sealed partial class Scenario04 : Page
25.     {
26.         private MainPage rootPage;
27.         private VideoMetaData video = null;
28.         private TimeSpan originalPosition = new TimeSpan(0);
29.         public Scenario04()
30.         {
31.             this.InitializeComponent();
32. 
33.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
34.             rootPage.SizeChanged += RootPage_SizeChanged;
35.         }
36. 
37.         protected async override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
38.         {
39.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
40. 
41.             //Subscribe to player events
42.             player.MediaOpened += Player_MediaOpened;
43.             player.MediaFailed += Player_MediaFailed;
44.             player.CurrentStateChanged += Player_CurrentStateChanged;
45.             player.MediaEnded += Player_MediaEnded;
46. 
47.             if (e.Parameter is DialReceiverActivatedEventArgs)
48.             {
49.                 DialReceiverActivatedEventArgs activationArgs = (DialReceiverActivatedEventArgs)e.Parameter;
50. 
51.                 //Parse the DIAL arguments from the activation arguments
52.                 DialLaunchArguments dialArgs = DialLaunchArguments.Parse(activationArgs.Arguments);
53. 
54.                 // Get the list of available Azure videos.
55.                 AzureDataProvider dataProvider = new AzureDataProvider();
56.                 // Get the vide that is playing
57.                 video = dataProvider.GetFromID(dialArgs.VideoId);
58.                 //Set the source on the player
59.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Opening '{0}'", video.Title), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
60.                 this.player.Source = video.VideoLink;
61.                 this.LicenseText.Text = "License: " + video.License;
62. 
63.                 player.Position = dialArgs.Position;
64.             }
65.             else
66.             {
67.                 StorageFile localVideo = await StorageFile.GetFileFromApplicationUriAsync(new Uri("ms-appx:///Assets/casting.mp4"));
68.                 var stream = await localVideo.OpenAsync(FileAccessMode.Read);
69.                 this.player.SetSource(stream, localVideo.FileType);
70.             }
71.         }
72. 
73.         private void Player_MediaEnded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
74.         {
75.             if (video == null)
76.             {
77.                 this.player.Position = new TimeSpan(0);
78.                 this.player.Play();
79.             }
80.         }

83.         {
84.             this.player.Height = this.Height;
85.             this.player.Width = this.Width;
86.         }

91.         {

93.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("{0}", this.player.CurrentState), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
94.             else
95.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("{0} '{1}'", this.player.CurrentState, video.Title), NotifyType.StatusMessage);

111.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Openend '{0}'", "Title"), NotifyType.StatusMessage);

30.     public sealed partial class Scenario05 : Page
31.     {
32.         private MainPage rootPage;
33.         private DevicePicker picker;
34.         private VideoMetaData video = null;
35.         ProjectionViewBroker pvb = new ProjectionViewBroker();
36.         DeviceInformation activeDevice = null;
37. 
38.         int thisViewId;
39. 
40.         public Scenario05()
41.         {
42.             this.InitializeComponent();
43. 
44.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
45. 
46.             //Subscribe to player events
47.             player.MediaOpened += Player_MediaOpened;
48.             player.MediaFailed += Player_MediaFailed;
49.             player.CurrentStateChanged += Player_CurrentStateChanged;
50. 
51.             // Get an Azure hosted video
52.             AzureDataProvider dataProvider = new AzureDataProvider();
53.             video = dataProvider.GetRandomVideo();
54. 
55.             //Set the source on the player
56.             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Opening '{0}'", video.Title), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
57.             this.player.Source = video.VideoLink;
58.             this.LicenseText.Text = "License: " + video.License;
59. 
60.             //Subscribe for the clicked event on the custom cast button
61.             ((MediaTransportControlsWithCustomCastButton)this.player.TransportControls).CastButtonClicked += TransportControls_CastButtonClicked;
62. 
63.             // Instantiate the Device Picker
64.             picker = new DevicePicker ();
65. 
66.             // Get the device selecter for Miracast devices
67.             picker.Filter.SupportedDeviceSelectors.Add(ProjectionManager.GetDeviceSelector());
68. 
69.             //Hook up device selected event
70.             picker.DeviceSelected += Picker_DeviceSelected;
71. 
72.             //Hook up device disconnected event
73.             picker.DisconnectButtonClicked += Picker_DisconnectButtonClicked;
74. 
75.             //Hook up picker dismissed event
76.             picker.DevicePickerDismissed += Picker_DevicePickerDismissed;
77. 
78.             // Hook up the events that are received when projection is stoppped
79.             pvb.ProjectionStopping += Pvb_ProjectionStopping;
80.         }
81. 
82.         private void TransportControls_CastButtonClicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
83.         {
84.             rootPage.NotifyUser("Custom Cast Button Clicked", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
85. 
86.             //Pause Current Playback
87.             player.Pause();
88. 
89.             //Get the custom transport controls
90.             MediaTransportControlsWithCustomCastButton mtc = (MediaTransportControlsWithCustomCastButton)this.player.TransportControls;
91. 
92.             //Retrieve the location of the casting button
93.             // TODO[migrate-011]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
94.             GeneralTransform transform = mtc.CastButton.TransformToVisual(Window.Current.Content as UIElement);
95.             Point pt = transform.TransformPoint(new Point(0, 0));
96. 
97.             //Show the picker above our custom cast button
98.             picker.Show(new Rect(pt.X, pt.Y, mtc.CastButton.ActualWidth, mtc.CastButton.ActualHeight), Windows.UI.Popups.Placement.Above);
99.         }
100.         private async void Picker_DeviceSelected(DevicePicker sender, DeviceSelectedEventArgs args)
101.         {       
102.             //Casting must occur from the UI thread.  This dispatches the casting calls to the UI thread.
103.             // TODO[migrate-012]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
104.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>
105.             {
106.                 try
107.                 { 
108.                 // Set status to Connecting
109.                 picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.SelectedDevice, "Connecting", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.ShowProgress);
110.                 
111.                 // Getting the selected device improves debugging
112.                 DeviceInformation selectedDevice = args.SelectedDevice;
113. 
114.                 // TODO[migrate-013]: see PATTERNS.md#getforcurrentview
115.                 thisViewId = Windows.UI.ViewManagement.ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().Id;
116. 
117.                 // If projection is already in progress, then it could be shown on the monitor again
118.                 // Otherwise, we need to create a new view to show the presentation
119.                 if (rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl == null)
120.                 {
121.                     // First, create a new, blank view
122.                     // TODO[migrate-014]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
123.                     var thisDispatcher = Window.Current.Dispatcher;
124.                     // TODO[migrate-015]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
125.                     await CoreApplication.CreateNewView().Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
126.                     {
127.                         // ViewLifetimeControl is a wrapper to make sure the view is closed only
128.                         // when the app is done with it
129.                         rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl = ViewLifetimeControl.CreateForCurrentView();
130. 
131.                         // Assemble some data necessary for the new page
132.                         pvb.MainPageDispatcher = thisDispatcher;
133.                         pvb.ProjectionViewPageControl = rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl;
134.                         pvb.MainViewId = thisViewId;
135. 
136.                         // Display the page in the view. Note that the view will not become visible
137.                         // until "StartProjectingAsync" is called
138.                         var rootFrame = new Frame();
139.                         rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(ProjectionViewPage), pvb);
140.                         // TODO[migrate-016]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
141.                         Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
142. 
143.                         // TODO[migrate-017]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
144.                         Window.Current.Activate();
145.                     });
146.                 }
147. 
148.                 try
149.                 {
150.                     // Start/StopViewInUse are used to signal that the app is interacting with the
151.                     // view, so it shouldn't be closed yet, even if the user loses access to it
152.                     rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl.StartViewInUse();
153. 
154.                     try
155.                     {
156.                         await ProjectionManager.StartProjectingAsync(rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl.Id, thisViewId, selectedDevice);
157. 
158.                     }
159.                     catch (Exception ex)
160.                     {
161.                         if (!ProjectionManager.ProjectionDisplayAvailable || pvb.ProjectedPage == null)
162.                             throw ex;
163.                     }
164. 
165.                     // ProjectionManager currently can throw an exception even when projection has started.\
166.                     // Re-throw the exception when projection has not been started after calling StartProjectingAsync 
167.                     if (ProjectionManager.ProjectionDisplayAvailable && pvb.ProjectedPage != null)
168.                     {
169.                         this.player.Pause();
170.                         await pvb.ProjectedPage.SetMediaSource(this.player.Source, this.player.Position);
171.                         activeDevice = selectedDevice;
172.                         // Set status to Connected
173.                         picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.SelectedDevice, "Connected", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.ShowDisconnectButton);
174.                         picker.Hide(); 
175.                     }
176.                     else
177.                     {
178.                         rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Projection has failed to '{0}'", selectedDevice.Name), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
179.                         // Set status to Failed
180.                         picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.SelectedDevice, "Connection Failed", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.ShowRetryButton);
181.                     }
182.                 }
183.                 catch (Exception)
184.                 {
185.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Projection has failed to '{0}'", selectedDevice.Name), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
186.                     // Set status to Failed
187.                     try { picker.SetDisplayStatus(args.SelectedDevice, "Connection Failed", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.ShowRetryButton); } catch { }
188.                 }
189.                 }
190.                 catch (Exception ex)
191.                 {
192.                     UnhandledExceptionPage.ShowUnhandledException(ex);
193.                 }
194.             });
195.         }
196.         private async void Picker_DevicePickerDismissed(DevicePicker sender, object args)
197.         {
198.             //Casting must occur from the UI thread.  This dispatches the casting calls to the UI thread.
199.             // TODO[migrate-018]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
200.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
201.             {
202.                 if (activeDevice == null)
203.                 {
204.                     player.Play();
205.                 }
206.             });
207.         }
208.         private async void Picker_DisconnectButtonClicked(DevicePicker sender, DeviceDisconnectButtonClickedEventArgs args)
209.         {
210.             //Casting must occur from the UI thread.  This dispatches the casting calls to the UI thread.
211.             // TODO[migrate-019]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
212.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
213.             {
214.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Disconnect Button clicked", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
215.                 //Update the display status for the selected device.
216.                 sender.SetDisplayStatus(args.Device, "Disconnecting", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.ShowProgress);
217. 
218.                 if (this.pvb.ProjectedPage != null)
219.                     this.pvb.ProjectedPage.StopProjecting();
220. 
221.                 //Update the display status for the selected device.
222.                 sender.SetDisplayStatus(args.Device, "Disconnected", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.None);
223.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Disconnected", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
224. 
225.                 // Set the active device variables to null
226.                 activeDevice = null;
227.             });
228.         }

230.         private async void Pvb_ProjectionStopping(object sender, EventArgs e)
231.         {
232.             ProjectionViewBroker broker = sender as ProjectionViewBroker;
233. 
234.             TimeSpan position;
235.             Uri source = null;
236. 
237.             // TODO[migrate-020]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
238.             await broker.ProjectedPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
239.             {
240.                 position = broker.ProjectedPage.Player.Position;
241.                 source = broker.ProjectedPage.Player.Source;
242.             });
243. 
244.             // TODO[migrate-021]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
245.             await rootPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
246.             {
247.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Resuming playback on the first screen", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
248.                 this.player.Source = source;
249. 
250.                 this.player.Position = position;
251.                 this.player.Play();
252.                 rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl = null;
253.             });
254.         }

68.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
69.         {
70.             broker = (ProjectionViewBroker)e.Parameter;
71.             broker.ProjectedPage = this;
72. 
73.             // Listen for when it's time to close this view
74.             broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.Released += thisViewControl_Released;

77.         private async void thisViewControl_Released(object sender, EventArgs e)
78.         {                        
79.             // There are two major cases where this event will get invoked:
80.             // 1. The view goes unused for some time, and the system cleans it up
81.             // 2. The app calls "StopProjectingAsync"
82.             broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.Released -= thisViewControl_Released;
83.             // TODO[migrate-103]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
84.             await broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
85.                 {
86.                     this.broker.NotifyProjectionStopping();
87.                     MainPage.Current.ProjectionViewPageControl = null;
88.                 });
89. 
90.             this._player.Stop();
91.             this._player.Source = null;
92. 
93.             // TODO[migrate-104]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
94.             Window.Current.Close();
95.         }
96. 
97.         public async void SwapViews()
98.         {
99.             // The view might arrive on the wrong display. The user can
100.             // easily swap the display on which the view appears
101.             broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.StartViewInUse();
102.             await ProjectionManager.SwapDisplaysForViewsAsync(
103.                 // TODO[migrate-105]: see PATTERNS.md#getforcurrentview
104.                 ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().Id,
105.                 broker.MainViewId
106.             );
107.             broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.StopViewInUse();                       
108.         }

110.         {

114.         public async void StopProjecting()
115.         {
116.             broker.NotifyProjectionStopping();
117.            
118.             // There may be cases to end the projection from the projected view
119.             // (e.g. the presentation hosted in that view concludes)
120.             // broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.StartViewInUse();
121.             // TODO[migrate-106]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
122.             await this._player.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>
123.             {
124.                 this._player.Stop();
125.                 this._player.Source = null;
126. 
127.                 broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.StartViewInUse();
128. 
129.                 try
130.                 {
131.                     await ProjectionManager.StopProjectingAsync(
132.                         broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.Id,
133.                         broker.MainViewId                     
134.                         );
135.                 }
136.                 catch { }
137.                 // TODO[migrate-107]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
138.                 Window.Current.Close();
139. 
140.             });
141.             
142.            broker.ProjectionViewPageControl.StopViewInUse();           
143.         }

178.         private async void Picker_DisconnectButtonClicked(DevicePicker sender, DeviceDisconnectButtonClickedEventArgs args)
179.         {
180.             //Casting must occur from the UI thread.  This dispatches the casting calls to the UI thread.
181.             // TODO[migrate-026]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
182.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>
183.             {
184.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Disconnect Button clicked", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
185.

192.                 if (this.activeCastConnectionHandler is DialApp)
193.                     disconnected = await TryStopDialAppAsync((DialApp)activeCastConnectionHandler);

196.

198.                 {

200.                     try { sender.SetDisplayStatus(args.Device, "Disconnected", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.None); } catch { }
201.                     // Set the active device variables to null
202.                     activeDevice = null;

207.                 }
208.                 else
209.                 {
210.                     //Update the display status for the selected device.
211.                     sender.SetDisplayStatus(args.Device, "Disconnect failed", DevicePickerDisplayStatusOptions.ShowDisconnectButton);
212.                 }
213.             });

218.         #region ProjectionManager APIs
219.         private async Task<bool> TryProjectionManagerCastAsync(DeviceInformation device)
220.         {
221.             bool projectionManagerCastAsyncSucceeded = false;
222. 
223.             if ((activeDevice ==null && ProjectionManager.ProjectionDisplayAvailable && device == null) || device != null)
224.             {
225.                 // TODO[migrate-027]: see PATTERNS.md#getforcurrentview
226.                 thisViewId = Windows.UI.ViewManagement.ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().Id;
227. 
228.                 // If projection is already in progress, then it could be shown on the monitor again
229.                 // Otherwise, we need to create a new view to show the presentation
230.                 if (rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl == null)
231.                 {
232.                     // First, create a new, blank view
233.                     // TODO[migrate-028]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
234.                     var thisDispatcher = Window.Current.Dispatcher;
235.                     // TODO[migrate-029]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
236.                     await CoreApplication.CreateNewView().Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
237.                     {
238.                         // ViewLifetimeControl is a wrapper to make sure the view is closed only
239.                         // when the app is done with it
240.                         rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl = ViewLifetimeControl.CreateForCurrentView();
241. 
242.                         // Assemble some data necessary for the new page
243.                         pvb.MainPageDispatcher = thisDispatcher;
244.                         pvb.ProjectionViewPageControl = rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl;
245.                         pvb.MainViewId = thisViewId;
246.                         
247.                         // Display the page in the view. Note that the view will not become visible
248.                         // until "StartProjectingAsync" is called
249.                         var rootFrame = new Frame();
250.                         rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(ProjectionViewPage), pvb);
251.                         // TODO[migrate-030]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
252.                         Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
253.                         // TODO[migrate-031]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
254.                         Window.Current.Activate();
255.                     });
256.                 }
257. 
258.                 try
259.                 {
260.                     // Start/StopViewInUse are used to signal that the app is interacting with the
261.                     // view, so it shouldn't be closed yet, even if the user loses access to it
262.                     rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl.StartViewInUse();
263. 
264.                     try
265.                     {
266.                         rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Starting projection of '{0}' on a second view '{1}' using ProjectionManager", video.Title, device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
267. 
268.                         await ProjectionManager.StartProjectingAsync(rootPage.ProjectionViewPageControl.Id, thisViewId, device);
269.                     }
270.                     catch (Exception ex)
271.                     {
272.                         if (!ProjectionManager.ProjectionDisplayAvailable)
273.                             throw ex;
274.                     }
275. 
276.                     if (pvb.ProjectedPage != null)
277.                     {
278.                         this.player.Pause();
279.                         await pvb.ProjectedPage.SetMediaSource(this.player.Source, this.player.Position);
280.                     }
281.                     if (device != null)
282.                     {
283.                         activeDevice = device;
284.                         activeCastConnectionHandler = pvb;
285.                     }
286.                     projectionManagerCastAsyncSucceeded = true;
287. 
288.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Displaying '{0}' on a second view '{1}' using ProjectionManager", video.Title, device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
289.                 }
290.                 catch (Exception)
291.                 {
292.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("The projection view is being disposed", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
293.                 }
294.                 // TODO[migrate-032]: see PATTERNS.md#getforcurrentview
295.                 ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().ExitFullScreenMode();
296.             }
297.                 return projectionManagerCastAsyncSucceeded;
298.         }

302.             return true;

306.         private async void Pvb_ProjectionStopping(object sender, EventArgs e)
307.         {
308.             ProjectionViewBroker broker = sender as ProjectionViewBroker;
309. 
310.             TimeSpan position;
311.             Uri source = null;

314.             await broker.ProjectedPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
315.             {
316.                 position = broker.ProjectedPage.Player.Position;
317.                 source = broker.ProjectedPage.Player.Source;
318.             });
319. 
320.             // TODO[migrate-034]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
321.             await rootPage.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
322.             {
323.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Resuming playback on the first screen", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
324.                 this.player.Source = source;
325.                 this.player.Position = position;
326.                 this.player.Play();

335.         private async Task<bool> TryCastMediaElementAsync(DeviceInformation device)
336.         {
337.             bool castMediaElementSucceeded = false;
338. 
339.             //Verify whether the selected device supports DLNA, Bluetooth, or Miracast.
340.             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Checking to see if device {0} supports Miracast, Bluetooth, or DLNA", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
341. 
342.             CastingConnection connection = null;
343. 
344.             //Check to see whether we are casting to the same device
345.             if (activeDevice != null && device.Id == activeDevice.Id)
346.                 connection = activeCastConnectionHandler as CastingConnection;
347.             else // if not casting to the same device reset the active device related variables.
348.             {
349.                 activeDevice = null;
350.                 activeCastConnectionHandler = null;
351.             }
352. 
353.             // If we can re-use the existing connection
354.             if (connection == null || connection.State == CastingConnectionState.Disconnected || connection.State == CastingConnectionState.Disconnecting)
355.             {
356.                 CastingDevice castDevice = null;
357.                 activeDevice = null;
358. 
359.                 //Try to create a CastingDevice instannce. If it doesn't succeed, the selected device does not support playback of the video source.
360.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Attempting to resolve casting device for '{0}'", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
361.                 try { castDevice = await CastingDevice.FromIdAsync(device.Id); } catch { }
362. 
363.                 if (castDevice == null)
364.                 {
365.                     //Try to create a DIAL device. If it doesn't succeed, the selected device does not support DIAL.
366.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("'{0}' does not support playback of this media", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
367.                 }
368.                 else
369.                 {
370.                     //Create a casting conneciton from our selected casting device
371.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Creating connection for '{0}'", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
372.                     connection = castDevice.CreateCastingConnection();
373. 
374.                     //Hook up the casting events
375.                     connection.ErrorOccurred += Connection_ErrorOccurred;
376.                     connection.StateChanged += Connection_StateChanged;
377.                 }
378. 
379.                 //Cast the content loaded in the media element to the selected casting device
380.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Casting to '{0}'", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
381. 
382.                 CastingSource source = null;
383.                 // Get the casting source
384.                 try { source = player.GetAsCastingSource(); } catch { }
385. 
386.                 if (source == null)
387.                 {
388.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Failed to get casting source for video '{0}'", video.Title), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
389.                 }
390.                 else
391.                 {
392.                     CastingConnectionErrorStatus status = await connection.RequestStartCastingAsync(source);
393. 
394.                     if (status == CastingConnectionErrorStatus.Succeeded)
395.                     {
396.                         //Remember the device to which casting succeeded
397.                         activeDevice = device;
398.                         //Remember the current active connection.
399.                         activeCastConnectionHandler = connection;
400.                         castMediaElementSucceeded = true;

--- RESULT ---
400.                         castMediaElementSucceeded = true;
401.                         player.Play();
402.                     }
403.                     else
404.                     {
405.                         rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Failed to cast to '{0}'", device.Name), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
406.                     }
407.                 }
408.             }
409.             return castMediaElementSucceeded;

429.         private async Task<bool> TryDisconnectCastingSessionAsync(CastingConnection connection)
430.         {
431.             bool disconnected = false;
432. 
433.             //Disconnect the casting session
434.             CastingConnectionErrorStatus status = await connection.DisconnectAsync();
435. 
436.             if (status == CastingConnectionErrorStatus.Succeeded)
437.             {
438.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Connection disconnected successfully.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
439.                 disconnected = true;
440.             }
441.             else
442.             {
443.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Failed to disconnect connection with reason {0}.", status.ToString()), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
444.             }
445. 
446.             return disconnected;
447.         }

451.         #region Windows.Media.DialProtocol APIs
452.         private async Task<bool> TryLaunchDialAppAsync(DeviceInformation device)
453.         {            
454.             bool dialAppLaunchSucceeded = false;
455. 
456.             //Update the launch arguments to include the Position
457.             this.dial_launch_args_textbox.Text = string.Format("v={0}&t={1}&pairingCode=E4A8136D-BCD3-45F4-8E49-AE01E9A46B5F", video.Id, player.Position.Ticks);
458. 
459.             //Try to create a DIAL device. If it doesn't succeed, the selected device does not support DIAL.
460.             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Checking to see if device {0} supports DIAL", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
461. 
462.             DialDevice dialDevice = null;
463. 
464.             //Try to create a DIAL device. If it doesn't succeed, the selected device does not support DIAL.
465.             rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Attempting to resolve DIAL device for '{0}'", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
466. 
467.             string[] newIds = (string[])device.Properties["{0BBA1EDE-7566-4F47-90EC-25FC567CED2A} 2"];
468. 
469.             if (newIds.Length > 0)
470.             {
471.                 string deviceId = newIds[0];
472.                 try { dialDevice = await DialDevice.FromIdAsync(deviceId); } catch { }
473.             }
474. 
475.             if (dialDevice == null)
476.             {
477.                 //Try to create a DIAL device. If it doesn't succeed, the selected device does not support DIAL.
478.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("'{0}' does not support DIAL", device.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
479.             }
480.             else
481.             {
482.                 //Get the DialApp object for the specific application on the selected device
483.                 DialApp app = dialDevice.GetDialApp(this.dial_appname_textbox.Text);
484. 
485.                 if (app == null)
486.                 {
487.                     //Try to create a DIAL device. If it doesn't succeed, the selected device does not support DIAL.
488.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("'{0}' cannot find app with ID '{1}'", device.Name, this.dial_appname_textbox.Text), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
489.                 }
490.                 else
491.                 {
492.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Attempting to launch '{0}'", app.AppName), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
493.                     //Launch the application on the 1st screen device
494.                     DialAppLaunchResult result = await app.RequestLaunchAsync(this.dial_launch_args_textbox.Text);
495. 
496.                     //Verify to see whether the application was launched
497.                     if (result == DialAppLaunchResult.Launched)
498.                     {
499.                         //Remember the device to which casting succeeded
500.                         activeDevice = device;
501.                         //DIAL is sessionsless but the DIAL app allows us to get the state and "disconnect".
502.                         //Disconnect in the case of DIAL is equivalenet to stopping the app.
503.                         activeCastConnectionHandler = app;
504.                         rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Launched '{0}'", app.AppName), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
505.                         //This is where you will need to add you application specific communication between your 1st and 2nd screen applications
506.                         //...
507. 
508. 
509.                         dialAppLaunchSucceeded = true;
510.                     }
511.                 }
512.             }
513. 
514.             return dialAppLaunchSucceeded;
515.         }
516.         private async Task<bool> TryStopDialAppAsync(DialApp app)
517.         {
518.             bool stopped = false;
519. 
520.             //Get the current application state
521.             DialAppStateDetails stateDetails = await app.GetAppStateAsync();
522. 
523.             switch (stateDetails.State)
524.             {
525.                 case DialAppState.NetworkFailure:
526.                     {
527.                         // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
528.                         rootPage.NotifyUser("Network Failure while getting application state", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
529.                         break;
530.                     }
531.                 case DialAppState.Stopped:
532.                     {
533.                         stopped = true;
534.                         // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
535.                         rootPage.NotifyUser("Application was already stopped.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
536.                         break;
537.                     }
538.                 default:
539.                     {
540.                         DialAppStopResult result = await app.StopAsync();
541. 
542.                         if (result == DialAppStopResult.Stopped)
543.                         {
544.                             stopped = true;
545.                             // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
546.                             rootPage.NotifyUser("Application stopped successfully.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
547.                         }
548.                         else
549.                         {
550.                             if (result == DialAppStopResult.StopFailed || result == DialAppStopResult.NetworkFailure)
551.                             {
552.                                 // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
553.                                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Error occured trying to stop application. Status: '{0}'", result.ToString()), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
554.                             }
555.                             else //in case of DialAppStopResult.OperationNotSupported, there is not much more you can do. You could implement your own
556.                             // mechanism to stop the application on that device.
557.                             {
558.                                 stopped = true;
559.                                 // In case getting the application state failed because of a network failure, you could add retry logic
560.                                 rootPage.NotifyUser(string.Format("Stop is not supported by device: '{0}'", activeDevice.Name), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
561.                             }
562.                         }
563.                         break;
564.                     }
565.             }
566.             return stopped;
567.         }

Read `.validator-diagnostics.txt` if the validator fails.\n\nKey things to look out for:\n- `player.Source` needs to be `Windows.Media.Core.MediaSource` not `Uri`\n- `player.MediaPlayer.PlaybackSession.Position` needs to be `TimeSpan` \n- In Scenario04's `SetMediaSource`, use `Windows.Media.Core.MediaSource` for source parameter\n- The `ProjectionViewPage.SetMediaSource` takes a `Uri source, TimeSpan position` in the original call from Scenario05/06, but we removed those calls. They're in obj/bin folders so might not be compiled.\n\n## STEP 20: Check and fix ViewLifetimeControl\n\nThe `Util/ViewLifetimeControl.cs` file uses CoreWindow and CoreDispatcher which are not available in WinUI 3. It should be excluded from the build. Add this to the csproj:\n```xml\n<Compile Remove=\"Util\\ViewLifetimeControl.cs\"/>\n```\n\n## STEP 21: Run the validator\n\nAfter a clean build, run:\n```powershell\n& \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run9\\000_AdvancedCasting\\app\\.github\\skills\\winui-uwp-migration\\scripts\\Validate-UwpMigration.ps1\" -Target \"C:\\ado\\win-dev-skills-benchmark\\agent-benchmark\\results\\run9\\000_AdvancedCasting\\app\\AdvancedCasting\"\n```\n\nFix any failures and re-run until PASS.\n\n## IMPORTANT NOTES\n\n1. The `MediaPlayerElement.Source` property in WinUI 3 accepts `IMediaPlaybackSource` (like `MediaSource`) not a `Uri`. Always use `Windows.Media.Core.MediaSource.CreateFromUri(uri)`.\n\n2. `MediaPlayerElement` does NOT have properties like `.CurrentState`, `.Position`, `.Play()`, `.Pause()`, `.Stop()` directly - all of these go through `.MediaPlayer.PlaybackSession` or `.MediaPlayer`.\n\n3. The `player.TransportControls` still works for MediaPlayerElement (same API).\n\n4. When Scenario05's `Picker_DeviceSelected` tries to call `ProjectionManager.GetDeviceSelector()`, this may throw on WinUI 3 desktop. Wrap in try/catch.\n\n5. In Scenario04, there's no `casting.mp4` asset in the project. The OnNavigatedTo needs to handle the FileNotFoundException gracefully. The scenario will show an error message which is acceptable behavior.\n\n6. The `SetMediaSource` method in ProjectionViewPage is called from nowhere now (since we removed projection code from Scenario05/06). But it still needs to compile.\n\n7. For `ProjectionViewBroker.MainPageDispatcher`, we changed the type to `Microsoft.UI.Dispatching.DispatcherQueue?`. But it's only used in Scenario05/06's multi-view code which we removed. So it doesn't need to be set anywhere.\n\n8. The `UnhandledExceptionPage` shows a red background with status text - that's fine as it's just an error page.\n\n9. Watch for nullable reference warnings - `Nullable` is enabled in the project.\n\n10. The `Scenario04` `TimeSpan originalPosition` field is unused after we remove the DialReceiverActivatedEventArgs handling - remove it.\n"}

19. // The Blank Application template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402347&clcid=0x409
20. 
21. namespace ScreenCasting
22. {
23.     /// <summary>
24.     /// Provides application-specific behavior to supplement the default Application class.
25.     /// </summary>
26.     sealed partial class App : Application
27.     {
28.         /// <summary>
29.         /// Initializes the singleton application object.  This is the first line of authored code
30.         /// executed, and as such is the logical equivalent of main() or WinMain().
31.         /// </summary>
32.         public App()
33.         {
34.             this.InitializeComponent();
35.             this.Suspending += OnSuspending;
36.         }
37. 
38.         /// <summary>
39.         /// Invoked when the application is launched normally by the end user.  Other entry points
40.         /// will be used such as when the application is launched to open a specific file.
41.         /// </summary>
42.         /// <param name="e">Details about the launch request and process.</param>
43.         protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e)
44.         {
45. #if DEBUG
46.             if (System.Diagnostics.Debugger.IsAttached)
47.             {
48.                 this.DebugSettings.EnableFrameRateCounter = false;
49. 
50.             }
51. 
52. #endif
53.             Application.Current.UnhandledException += Current_UnhandledException;
54. 
55.             // TODO[migrate-037]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
56.             Window.Current.Activate();
57.                 
58.             // TODO[migrate-038]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
59.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
60. 
61.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
62.             // just ensure that the window is active
63.             if (rootFrame == null)
64.             {
65.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
66.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
67.                 // Set the default language
68.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
69. 
70.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
71. 
72.                 if (e.PreviousExecutionState == ApplicationExecutionState.Terminated)
73.                 {
74.                     //TODO: Load state from previously suspended application
75.                 }
76. 
77.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
78.                 // TODO[migrate-039]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
79.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
80.                 // TODO[migrate-040]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
81.                 Window.Current.Activate();
82.             }
83. 
84.             if (rootFrame.Content == null)
85.             {
86.                 // When the navigation stack isn't restored navigate to the first page,
87.                 // configuring the new page by passing required information as a navigation
88.                 // parameter                
89.                 try
90.                 {
91.                     rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage), e);
92.                 }
93.                 catch (Exception ex)
94.                 {
95.                     // TODO[migrate-041]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
96.                     Window.Current.Content = new Frame();
97.                     // TODO[migrate-042]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
98.                     Window.Current.Activate();
99.                     rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(UnhandledExceptionPage), e.Arguments);
100.                     ((UnhandledExceptionPage)rootFrame.Content).StatusMessage = ex.Message + ex.StackTrace;
101.                 }
102. 
103.             }
104.            
105. 
106.             // Ensure the current window is active
107.             // TODO[migrate-043]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
108.             Window.Current.Activate();
109.         }
110. 
111.         private void Current_UnhandledException(object sender, UnhandledExceptionEventArgs e)
112.         {
113.             // TODO[migrate-044]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
114.             Window.Current.Content = new Frame();
115.             // TODO[migrate-045]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
116.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
117.             // TODO[migrate-046]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
118.             Window.Current.Activate();
119.             rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(UnhandledExceptionPage));
120.             ((UnhandledExceptionPage)rootFrame.Content).StatusMessage = e.Exception.Message + e.Exception.StackTrace;
121.         }
122. 
123.         protected override void OnActivated(IActivatedEventArgs args)
124.         {
125.             try
126.             {
127.                 // TODO[migrate-047]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
128.                 Window.Current.Content = new Frame();
129.                 // TODO[migrate-048]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
130.                 Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
131.                 // TODO[migrate-049]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
132.                 Window.Current.Activate();
133. 
134. 
135.                 if (args.Kind == ActivationKind.DialReceiver)
136.                 {
137.                     rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage), args);
138.                 }
139.                 else
140.                 {
141.                     rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage));
142.                 }
143.                 base.OnActivated(args);
144.             }
145.             catch (Exception ex)
146.             {
147.                 // TODO[migrate-050]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
148.                 Window.Current.Content = new Frame();
149.                 // TODO[migrate-051]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
150.                 Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
151.                 // TODO[migrate-052]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
152.                 Window.Current.Activate();
153.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(UnhandledExceptionPage));
154.                 ((UnhandledExceptionPage)rootFrame.Content).StatusMessage = ex.Message + ex.StackTrace;
155.             }
156.         }
157. 
158.         /// <summary>
159.         /// Invoked when Navigation to a certain page fails
160.         /// </summary>
161.         /// <param name="sender">The Frame which failed navigation</param>
162.         /// <param name="e">Details about the navigation failure</param>
163.         void OnNavigationFailed(object sender, NavigationFailedEventArgs e)
164.         {
165.             throw new Exception("Failed to load Page " + e.SourcePageType.FullName);
166.         }
167. 
168.         /// <summary>
169.         /// Invoked when application execution is being suspended.  Application state is saved
170.         /// without knowing whether the application will be terminated or resumed with the contents
171.         /// of memory still intact.
172.         /// </summary>
173.         /// <param name="sender">The source of the suspend request.</param>
174.         /// <param name="e">Details about the suspend request.</param>
175.         private void OnSuspending(object sender, SuspendingEventArgs e)
176.         {
177.             var deferral = e.SuspendingOperation.GetDeferral();
178.             //TODO: Save application state and stop any background activity
179.             deferral.Complete();
180.         }
181.     }
182. }

26. {
27.     /// <summary>
28.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
29.     /// </summary>
30.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
31.     {
32.         public static MainPage Current;
33.         public ViewLifetimeControl ProjectionViewPageControl;
34.         public MainPage()
35.         {
36.             LoadScenarios();
37. 
38.             this.DataContext = this;
39. 
40.             this.InitializeComponent();
41. 
42.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
43.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
44.             Current = this;
45.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
46.         }
47. 
48.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
49.         {
50.             // TODO[migrate-054]: see PATTERNS.md#getforcurrentview
51.             ApplicationView.GetForCurrentView().ExitFullScreenMode();
52.             this.ScenarioControl.SelectionChanged += ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged;
53. 
54.             try
55.             {
56.                 // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
57.                 ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = Scenarios;
58.                 
59.                 if (e.Parameter is DialReceiverActivatedEventArgs)
60.                 {
61.                     NavigateToScenario(typeof(Scenario04), e.Parameter);
62.                 }
63.                 else
64.                 {
65.                     ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
66.                 }
67. 
68.             }
69.             catch (Exception ex)
70.             {
71.                 // TODO[migrate-055]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
72.                 Window.Current.Content = new Frame();
73.                 // TODO[migrate-056]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
74.                 Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
75.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(UnhandledExceptionPage), null);
76.                 ((UnhandledExceptionPage)rootFrame.Content).StatusMessage = ex.Message + ex.StackTrace;
77.             }
78.         }
79. 
80.         /// <summary>
81.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
82.         /// sample scenario page.
83.         /// </summary>
84.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
85.         /// <param name="e"></param>
86.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
87.         {
88.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
89.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
90. 
91.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
92.             Scenario s = Scenarios[scenarioListBox.SelectedIndex];
93.             if (s != null)
94.             {
95.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
96.                 // TODO[migrate-057]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
97.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
98.                 {
99.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
100.                     StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
101.                 }
102.                 else
103.                 {
104.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = true;
105.                     StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
106.                 }
107.             }
108.         }
109. 
110. 
111.       
112.         internal void NavigateToScenario(Type scenarioType, object args)
113.         {
114.             try
115.             {
116.                 // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
117.                 NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
118. 
119.                 ListBox scenarioListBox = this.ScenarioControl;
120. 
121.                 int selectedIndex = -1;
122. 
123.                 this.ScenarioControl.SelectionChanged -= ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged;
124.                 for (int idx = 0; idx < scenarioListBox.Items.Count; idx++)
125.                 {
126.                     if (((Scenario)scenarioListBox.Items[idx]).ClassType == scenarioType)
127.                     {
128.                         selectedIndex = idx;
129.                         break;
130.                     }
131.                 }
132. 
133.                 if (selectedIndex > -1)
134.                     scenarioListBox.SelectedIndex = selectedIndex;
135. 
136.                 Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
137. 
138.                 if (s != null)
139.                 {
140.                     ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType, args);
141.                     // TODO[migrate-058]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
142.                     if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
143.                     {
144.                         Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
145.                         StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
146.                     }
147.                     else
148.                     {
149.                         Splitter.IsPaneOpen = true;
150.                         StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
151.                     }
152.                 }
153.                 this.ScenarioControl.SelectionChanged += ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged;
154.             }
155.             catch(Exception ex)
156.             {
157.                 // TODO[migrate-059]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
158.                 Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
159. 
160.                 // TODO[migrate-060]: see PATTERNS.md#windowing
161.                 Window.Current.Content = new Frame();
162.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(UnhandledExceptionPage), ex.Message);
163.                 ((UnhandledExceptionPage)rootFrame.Content).StatusMessage = ex.Message + ex.StackTrace;
164.             }
165.         }
166. 
167.     
168.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
169.         {
170.             get { return this.scenarios; }
171.         }
172. 
173.         /// <summary>
174.         /// Used to display messages to the user
175.         /// </summary>
176.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
177.         /// <param name="type"></param>
178.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
179.         {
180.             switch (type)
181.             {
182.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
183.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
184.                     break;
185.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
186.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
187.                     break;
188.             }
189.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
190. 
191.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
192.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
193.         }
194. 
195.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
196.         {
197.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
198.         }
199. 
200.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
201.         {
202.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = (Splitter.IsPaneOpen == true) ? false : true;
203.             StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
204.         }
205.     }
206.     public enum NotifyType
207.     {
208.         StatusMessage,
209.         ErrorMessage
210.     };

12. // The objects defined here demonstrate how to make sure each of the views created remains alive as long as 
13. // the app needs them, but only when they're being used by the app or the user. Many of the scenarios contained in this
14. // sample use these functions to keep track of the views available and ensure that the view is not closed while
15. // the scenario is attempting to show it.
16. //
17. // As you can see in scenario 1, the ApplicationViewSwitcher.TryShowAsStandaloneAsync and 
18. // ProjectionManager.StartProjectingAsync methods let you show one view next to another. The Consolidated event
19. // is fired when a view stops being visible separately from other views. Common cases where this will occur
20. // is when the view falls out of the list of recently used apps, or when the user performs the close gesture on the view.
21. // This is a good time to close the view, provided the app isn't trying to show the view at the same time. This event
22. // is fired on the thread of the view that becomes consolidated.
23. //
24. // Each view lives on its own thread, so concurrency control is necessary. Also, as you'll see in the sample,
25. // certain objects may be bound to UI on given threads. Properties of those objects should only be updated
26. // on that UI thread.
